dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ
vyāsa uvāca
phālgunebrāhmaṇaśreṣṭhaśrīkṛṣṇaṃsuravaṃditam
pūjayedbhaktibhāvenapratyahaṃvaiṣṇavojanaḥ 1
phālgunesnāpayedyastusarpiṣādevakīsutam
phalaṃtasyapravakṣyāmiyataḥ samyaṅinaśāmaya 2
sarvayajñaphalaṃprāpyasarvadānaphalaṃtathā
aṃteyātihareḥsthānaṃsarvapāpavivarjitaḥ 3
yugakoṭisahasrāṇibhukttvābhogaṃharergṛhe
tatraivamokṣamāpnotisaṃprāpyajñānamuttamam 4
yastuyacchatikṛṣṇāyaśiśiregopamūrtaye
tilānāṃmodakaṃdivyaṃsagaccheddharimaṃdiram 5
yodugdhalaḍḍukāndadyātkeśavāyamahātmane
sapibedamṛtaṃsvargemanvaṃtaraśatāvadhi 6
harayelalitaṃkhaṃḍaṃyastuyacchatijaimine
tasyaviṣṇuḥprasannātmāchinattibhavabaṃdhanam 7
vicitraṃphalitaṃdivyaṃdadyādbhagavatedvija
aṃteśakrapuraṃgatvāsabhavetsuravaṃditaḥ 8
nirmalāṃśarkarāyacchedyastukṛṣṇāyabhaktimān
sakiṃnalabhatevipravāsudevaprasādataḥ 9
supakvaṃphālgunemāsimadhuraṃbadarīphalam
yastuyacchatikṛṣṇāyaphalaṃtasyaniśāmaya 10
ihabhuṅktesukhaṃsarvaṃputrapautrasamanvitaḥ
aṃteharergṛhaṃyātirathamāruhyaśobhanam 11
nadadyādguḍasaṃyuktaṃharayebadarīphalam
ajñānādbrāhmaṇaśreṣṭhadadyāccennārakībhavet 12
phālgunemāsiyodadyāddharayedāḍimīphalam
supakvaṃtatphalaṃvipravadatomeniśāmaya 13
tatrayāvaṃtibījānitiṣṭhaṃtidāḍimīphale
tāvanmanvaṃtaraṃviṣṇorgṛhetiṣṭhatibhāgyavān 14
phālgunemāsiyodadyāddharayeguḍapiṣṭakam
savijñeyodvijaśreṣṭhavājimedhasahasrakṛt 15
caitremāsidvijaśreṣṭhamadhunāmadhusūdanam
snāpayellabhatemartyastadviṣṇoḥparamaṃpadam 16
madhunāsnāpayedyastunārāyaṇamanāmayam
nacarcākriyatetasyakadācidra visūnunā 17
caitrekiṃśukapuṣpeṇayo'cayetkamalāpatim
tannāmacitraguptenapaṃjikāyāṃnalikhyate 18
caitrakejagatāmīśaṃkṛṣṇaṃtilakapuṣpakaiḥ
pūjatonāstivaijanmapunarasminmahītale 19
kṛṣṇavaṃjulapuṣpeṇasarvadevaśiromaṇim
pūjayanmanujovipralabhatenāpadaṃkvacit 20
vāsaṃtībhiḥ sugaṃdhībhirvasaṃteyastupūjayet
bhagavaṃtaṃprasannātmāsadevairapipūjyate 21
tathākisalayairdivyairakhaṇḍairyo'cayeddharim
taṃvaṃdatesamutthāyasvayaṃpīṭhāsanopica 22
dhātrīpatrairnavairyastukomalairharimarcayet
acireṇaivalabhatesakalaṃvāṃchitaṃjanaḥ 23
śāṃḍilyākhaṃḍapatraiścadhattūraiścārkapuṣpakaiḥ
yo'cayedviṣṇumīśaṃcasasaṃsārābdhipāragaḥ 24
yodadyādviṣṇaveviprakadalīphalamuttamam
śakrādyāstridaśāḥ sarvevaṃdaṃtetamaharniśam 25
yodadyāccaitrakemāsibhaktyāgopālarūpiṇe
godhūmapiṣṭakaṃviprasarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate 26
āyātemādhavemāsipavitremādhava priye
āmiṣaṃmaithunaṃtailaṃviṣṇubhaktaḥ parityajet 27
prāptaḥ samācaretsnānaṃmādhavemāsivaiṣṇavaḥ
parityajetparānnaṃcanakuryāccadvibhojanam 28
prabhātepūjayedviṣṇuṃpūrvoktavidhinādvija
vaiśākhesnāpayedviṣṇuṃpuṣpavāsitavāriṇā 29
snāpayecchītatoyeṣusaṃdhyāparyaṃtamacyutam
trisaṃdhyaṃpūjayedbhaktyānaivedyairvividhaiḥprabhum 30
vaiśākhedamanasragbhirlakṣmīpatiralaṃkṛtaḥ
nakiṃdadātiviprarṣeprasannaḥ parameśvaraḥ 31
vaiśākhemāsiyodadyādyavānnaṃcakrapāṇaye
tasyapuṇyānisaṃkhyātuṃkaḥ samartho'stipaṃḍitaḥ 32
yatkiṃcinmādhavemāsimādhavaprītihetave
dīyatemādhavāyaivatatsarvamakṣayaṃbhavet 33
yadanyatsukṛtaṃkarmakriyatemāsimādhave
mādhavaprītayeviprakṣayastasyanavidyate 34
vaiśākhemāsiyaḥ kuryātprapāṃmādhavatuṣṭaye
dinedine'śvamedhasyaphalamāpnotimānavaḥ 35
vaiśākhodurllabhomāsaḥ sarvakarmaphalapradaḥ
pūjitavyoharistatrahitvākarmaśatānyapi 36

aśvatthaṃpūjayedyastusaevaviṣṇupūjakaḥ
aśvatthamūrtirbhagavānsvayamevayatodvija 44
avajñānāddivajaśreṣṭhayo'śvatthaṃhaṃtimūḍhadhīḥ
saṃsārenāstitatkarmayatkṛtvāsacaśuddhyti 45
aśvatthovṛkṣarājo'yaharimūrtiḥ prakīrtitaḥ
tasmādaśvatthahaṃtṝṇāṃtrātāko'pinavidyate 46
aśvatthaṃpaśyatovipraspṛśataḥ paratastathā
dehasthaṃpātakaṃsarvaṃharetpraṇamatohariḥ 47
vilokyāśvatthahaṃtāraṃyaḥ śaktonanivārayet
tannetrayugmaṃbaḍiśairyamenotpāṭyatesvayam 48
aśvatthacchedanaṃmūḍhomākurvitivadennayaḥ
tasyajihvāṃchurikayāsvayaṃkṛṃtatipretarāṭ 49
aśvatthaśākhāmekāṃyaḥsvalpāmapinihaṃtivai
sakoṭibrahmahatyānāṃphalamāpnotimānavaḥ 50
yatpāpaṃbrahmahatyāyāṃgurustrīgamanenaca
surāpānetathāsteyenyāsāpaharaṇe'pica 51
yatpāpaṃbhrūṇahatyāyāṃgohatyāyāṃdvijottama
strīhatyāyāṃcayatpāpaṃparastrīgamanetathā 52
śaraṇāgata hatyāyāṃhatyāyāṃsuhṛdastathā
viśvāsavākyākathanepatihiṃsāvidhaucayat 53
yatpāpaṃparaniṃdāyāṃharivāsarabhojane
aśvatthacchedanāddhoraṃtatpāpaṃprāpyatejanaiḥ 54
viṣṇumūrtterjanomohādaśvatthasyanihaṃtiyaḥ
tattulyaḥ pātakīko'pinaśrutaḥkṣitimaṃḍale 55
vadāmyaśvatthamāhātmyaṃsarvapāpavināśanam
setihāsaṃdvijaśreṣṭhaśṛṇuvatsasamāhitaḥ 56
pūrvaṃdhanaṃjayonāmabrāhmaṇoharibhaktikṛt
āsīttretāyugeviprasarvaprāṇihiterataḥ 57
jñātipūjāratonityaṃdīpadānerataḥsadā
satyavādījitakrodhohiṃsādaṃbhavivarjitaḥ 58
mumukṣuḥsadvijaśreṣṭhasarvadāparameśvaram
pūjayāmāsaparayābhaktyānārāyaṇaṃprabhum 59
tasyabhaktiṃtatojñātvāmahatīṃsudṛḍhāṃprabhuḥ
jahārasakalaṃvittaṃhetumātreṇakenacit 60
tathāpisadvijaśreṣṭhaḥkeśavasyamahātmanaḥ
pūjāmanudinecakrebhaktyāparamayāsudhīḥ 61
duḥkhenopārjitaṃvittaṃvinaṣṭaṃsakalaṃdvija
dṛṣṭvāpitenavipreṇaduḥkhenāciṃtyacetasā 62
bhakṣaṇevarjanaṃcakresavipraḥ paramārthavit
mahāviṣṇusaparyāyāṃdṛḍhaṃbaddhvāmanonijam 63
bhūyo'pitasyaviprasya bhaktiṃjñātvājanārdanaḥ
cakārabaṃdhuvicchedaṃsudṛḍhaḥ śamadastataḥ 64
bāṃdhavāstasyaviprasyaviṣṇumāyāvimohitāḥ
hiṃsāmārebhirekartuṃsarvadaivadvijottama 65
tatoviprohinirvṛttonirbaṃdhaiḥ puruṣottamam
pūjayāmāsasatataṃprītaḥ pracurabhaktitaḥ 66

bhūyaevamahāviṣṇuḥkautukītasyajaimine
jahārasānukaṃpo'piputrānapidinedine 68
tathāpisadvijaśreṣṭhaḥkeśavaṃkleśanāśanam
pūrvadviguṇayābhaktyānityaṃviṣṇumapūjayat 69
tasyapatnītatovipraduḥkhaśokātiduḥkhitā
pitṛgehaṃgatāviṣṇormāyayāparimohitā 70
athaikākīsabhūdevoviṣṇubhaktiparāyaṇaḥ
vipadaṃciṃtayāmāsanakadāpisucetasā 71
ekadāsadvijaśreṣṭhaviṣṇubhaktimatāṃvaraḥ
skaṃdheparaśumādāyakāṣṭhārthaṃvipineyayau 72
vanātkāṣṭhaṃsamādāyanityamevacasadvijaḥ
himāgamevastrahīnaḥ kuruteśītavāraṇam 73
kadācidvipinaṃgaṃtuṃnāśaknoddivajasattamaḥ
jaghānaprāṃgaṇasthasyaśākhāmaśvatthaśākhinaḥ 74
atrāṃtare mahāviṣṇustasmādaśvatthapādapāt

dadarśaviṣṇuṃpurataḥ savipraścaturbhujaṃpadmadalāyatākṣam

parisravadvistararaktadhārāsaṃdhyāsuśoṇīkṛtanavyamegham

trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ
vyāsa uvāca
jyeṣṭhemāsidvijaśreṣṭhabhagavaṃtaṃjanārdanam
pūjayedbhaktibhāvenajalaiḥsaṃsnāpyaśītalaiḥ 1
udvartanaṃcadātavyaṃsugandhyāmalakaṃtathā
tailaṃsuṃgaṃdhaṃharayegrīṣmakāledinedine 2
suvāsiteśītalecamandire'timanorame
pratyahaṃkamalākāṃtaṃsthāpayejjanamaṃḍape 3
naraudra deśeviprendra sadhūmeiṃdhanālaye
nasūtikāgṛhecaivasthāpayetkamalāpatim 4
cāmarairvījitaḥśvetaiḥ sudīrghaiḥ kamalāpatim
jyeṣṭhemāsidvijaśreṣṭhasuprītaḥ kiṃnayacchati 5
mayūrapucchavyajanairnidārghairvījitohariḥ
dadātyabhimataṃsarvamacireṇaivasattama 6
tālavṛṃtakavātenapavitrāṃbaravāyunā
yaigrīṣmevījyateviṣṇustesarvesvargagāminaḥ 7
yogātralepanaṃkuryātsugandhīyaiścakardamaiḥ
grīṣmehariṃcaṃdanaiścasaviśenmādhavīṃtanum 8
uṣmāgamedvijaśreṣṭhasamuktonātrasaṃśayaḥ
praphullakusumodyānetulasīkānanetathā 9
saṃdhyāyāṃsthāpayedviṣṇuṃdeśedhīrasamīraṇe
sragbhiḥpāṭalapuṣpāṇāṃyenaviṣṇuralaṃkṛtaḥ 10
jyeṣṭhemāsisavijñeyoaśvamedhasahasrakṛt
yastumuktāvalidadyādgrīṣmevaiśrīpaterjanaḥ 11
bhūpālatvaṃharistasmaiyacchejjanmanijanmani
yastumaṇḍayatigrīṣmeśrīkṛṣṇaṃmaṇimālayā 12
tasyapuṇyaphalaṃvipravadatomeniśāmaya
yāvadbrahmāsṛjatyetajjaiminesakalaṃjagat 13
tiṣṭhedviṣṇupuretāvanmaṇimālāvibhūṣitaḥ
suvarṇābharaṇairyasturajatābharaṇaistathā 14
kṛṣṇaṃmaṃḍayatigrīṣmeso'pitatphalamāpnuyāt
vicitraṃyastuparyaṃkaṃsagaṃḍūkaṃprayacchati 15
harayedevadevāyanasaduḥkhīkadācana
grīṣmakālenadeyānigurūṇivasanānica 16
harayebrāhmaṇaśreṣṭhadeyaṃtanvaṃśuṃkaṃśuci
yastvacyutaphalairdivyaiḥsugaṃdhaiḥpūjayeddharim 17

datto'yatevaraḥ ko'pivipranāstyatrasaṃśayaḥ

nāmasaptadaśo'dhyāyaḥ 17

aṣṭādaśo'dhyāyaḥ
jaiminiruvāca
tīrthaśreṣṭhamitiproktaṃyattvayāpuruṣottamam
tanmāhātmyaṃgurobrūhiyaditemayyanugrahaḥ 1
vyāsauvāca
puruṣottamasyamāhātmyaṃsamāsenaśṛṇudvija
samyagvaktuṃjagatyasminkaḥśaktoviṣṇunāvinā 2
lavaṇāṃbhonidhestīraṃpuruṣottamasaṃjñitam
puraṃtadbrāhmaṇaśreṣṭhasvargādapicadurllabham 3
svayamastipuretasminyataḥ śrīpuruṣottamaḥ
puruṣottamamityuktaṃtasmāttannāmakovidaiḥ 4
kṣetraṃtaddurllabhaṃviprasamaṃtāddaśayojanam
tatrasthadehinodevairdṛśyaṃtecacaturbhujāḥ 5
praviśanvaitutatkṣetraṃsarvesyurviṣṇumūrtayaḥ
tasmādvicāraṇātatranakarttavyāvicakṣaṇaiḥ 6
cāṇḍālenāpisaṃspṛṣṭaṃgrāhyaṃtatrānnamagrajaiḥ
sākṣādviṣṇuryatastatracāṇḍālodvijasattama 7
tatrānnapācikālakṣmīḥsvayaṃbhoktājanārddanaḥ
tasmāttatraudanaṃvipradaivatairapidurllabham 8
haribhuktāvaśiṣṭaṃyatpavitraṃbhuvidurllabham
annaṃyebhuñjatelokāsteṣāṃmuktirnadurllabhā 9
brahmādyāstridaśāḥ sarvetadannamatidurllabham
bhuñjatenityamāgatyamānuṣāṇāṃcakākathā 10
nayasyaramatecittaṃtasminnannesudurllabhe
tamevaviṣṇudveṣṭāraṃprāhuḥsarvamaharṣayaḥ 11
pavitraṃbhuvisarvatrayathāgaṅgājalaṃdvija
tathāpavitraṃsarvatratadannaṃpāpanāśanam 12
tadannaṃkomalaṃdivyaṃyadyapidvijasattama
kṛkacasyodaraprāyaṃtathāpyaghavidāraṇe 13
pūrvārjitānipāpānikṣayaṃyāsyaṃtiyasyavai
bhaktiḥpravartateyasyatasminnannesudurllabhe 14
bahujanmārjitaṃpuṇyaṃyasyayāsyatisaṃkṣayam
tasminnannedvijaśreṣṭhatasyabhaktirnajāyate 15
indra dyumnasyasarasimārkaṃḍeyahradetathā
rohiṇyāṃcasamudre caśvetagaṅgājaleṣuca 16
snānaṃyekurvatemartyābhaktibhāvasamanvitāḥ
teṣāṃnavidyatejanmapunarasminmahītale 17
lavaṇāṃbhonidhestoyaiḥpitarastarpitādvija
sarvaduḥkhavinirmuktāvrajaṃtiharimaṃdiram 18
tīrtharājaḥsamudro 'saukīrtitastatvadarśibhiḥ
tasmāttatrakṛtaṃkarmasarvamevākṣayaṃbhavet 19
pitrarcanaṃtathādānaṃbhagavaccaraṇārcanam
japayajñaṃtathānyaccatasminkṣetremanorame 20
yatkarmakurutemartyoviṣṇuprīṇanahetave
sarvamevākṣayaṃtaccabhavennāstyatrasaṃśayaḥ 21
balabhadraṃ subhadrāṃ cakṛṣṇaṃcakamalekṣaṇam
yemānavāḥprapaśyaṃtiteṣāṃkiṃcinnadurllabham 22
adṛṣṭvāśrījagannāthaṃsubhadrāṃ cabalaṃtathā
mokṣaṃnalabhatemartyaḥkurvanpuṇyaśatānyapi 23
tatravetraprahāreṇaśarīraṃyasyalohitam
taṃvaṃdaṃtedvijaśreṣṭhadevāḥ śakrādayo'khilāḥ 24
sthitvāṃtarikṣeśakrādyāḥsarvadevagaṇādvija
vimānacāriṇo'nyonyaṃpravadaṃtītiharṣitāḥ 25aṃ
kadāmānuṣyamasmabhyaṃdāsyatekamalāpatiḥ
manuṣyaivagacchāmastadādra ṣṭuṃhariṃprabhum 26
kadāvetraprahāreṇakṣetreśrīpuruṣottame
bhaviṣyaṃtyasmadīyānilohitānivapūṃṣica 27
vāsavādyāḥsurāḥsarvetasminkṣetrevaraprade
sadāvetraprahārāṃścavāṃchaṃtidvijasattama 28
tatrākṣayavaṭaṃyetubhaktyāpaśyaṃtimānavāḥ
koṭijanmārjitaiḥ pāpairmuktāyāṃtiparāṃgatim 29
subhadrāṃ balabhadraṃ cajagannāthamanāmayam
śvetaṃmādhavadeveśaṃmārkaṃḍeyahradaṃtathā 30
jyāmeśvaraṃhanūmaṃtaṃtatrākṣayyaṃvaṭaṃdvija
paśyaṃtibhaktyāyemartyāsteṣāṃmuktirhiśāśvatī 31
dolāyamānaṃgovindaṃphālgunemāsitatraye
paśyaṃtimānavābhaktyāteṣāṃpuṇyaṃniśāmaya 32
vimuktāḥ sakalaiḥpāpairaṃteyāṃtiharergṛham
jñānaṃsaṃprāpyatatraivamokṣaṃyāṃtisudurllabham 33
cetrakemāsivāruṇyāṃyojagannāthamīkṣate
samṛtaḥpraviśeddehaṃjagannāthasyajaimine 34
vaiśākhecaivaśuklāyāmekādaśyāṃjagatpatim
tṛtīyāyāṃcayaḥ paśyenmuktaevasamānavaḥ 35
praviśedyastumanujomahāsnānaṃjagatpateḥ
tasyasiddhyaṃtiviprarṣesarvaevamanorathāḥ 36
brahmādyāstridaśāḥ sarvesthitvākāśejagatpateḥ
mahāsnānaṃprapaśyaṃtibhaktibhāvasamanvitāḥ 37
mahājyeṣṭhyāṃcaviprarṣejagannāthamanāmayam
ālokyalabhatemartyoviṣṇostatparamaṃpadam 38
guṃḍikāmaṇḍapaṃyāṃtamāṣāḍhekamalāpatim
balabhadraṃ cayaḥpaśyetsamuktonātrasaṃśayaḥ 39
yaḥ paśyatijagannāthaṃrathasthaṃkamalekṣaṇam
tasyanāstipunarjanmasaṃsāresarvaduḥkhade 40
rathārūḍhāṃsubhadrāṃ cabhaktyāpaśyaṃtimānavāḥ
chinattibhagavāṃstasya duḥkhadaṃbhavabaṃdhanam 41
aputrāmṛtaputrācayāsubhadrāṃ prapaśyati
bahvapatyājīvavatsāsānārībhavatidvija 42
durbhagākākavaṃdhyāvāsubhadrāṃ yāprapaśyati
sāsvāmisubhagānārībahvapatyābhavetkhalu 43
guṃḍikāmaṃḍapasthaṃcakṛṣṇaṃpaśyatiyaḥ pumān
balabhadraṃ subhadrāṃ casayātiparamaṃpadam 44
rogīduḥkhīcayaḥ paśyedguṇḍikāmaṃḍapeharim
rogācchokāccasahasājaiminesapramucyate 45
yastvaputrojagannāthaṃguṃḍikāmaṇḍapesthitam
paśyetsacadvijaśreṣṭhaputraṃprāpnotivaiṣṇavam 46
vidyārthīlabhatevidyāṃdhanārthīlabhatedhanam
dārārthīlabhatedārānmokṣārthīmokṣamāpnuyāt 47
bhraṣṭarājyonṛpoyastuhariṃpaśyatibhaktitaḥ
guṃḍikāmaṃḍapevipranijarājyaṃlabhetasaḥ 48
śatrubhirvarjitoyastuguṃḍikāmaṃḍapeharim
bhaktyāpaśyativiprarṣetasyanaśyaṃtiśatravaḥ 49
guṃḍikāmaṃḍapepaśyedyorājapīḍitobhṛśam
sasadyaevarājānaṃsvakīyaṃvaśamānayet 50
sarvāsāmevayātrāṇāṃguṃḍikāpravarāmatā
tasmātsamānavaiḥ kāryātyaktvākāryaśatānyapi 51
śayanecatathotthānetasminkṣetremanorame
hariṃpaśyatiyomartyaḥsadevrapipūjyate 52
puruṣottamamāhātmyavaktuṃśaknotikaḥkṣitau
yasyapraveśamātreṇanaronārāyaṇobhavet 53
bahunātrakimuktenasaṃkṣepāducyatemayā
sarveṣāmevatīrthānāṃvariṣṭhaṃpuruṣottamam 54
saṃsārasindhumatinimnamimaṃtitīrṣuḥ kleśapradaṃviṣamapāpagaṇāśrayaṃyaḥ
kṣetresamastasukhadepuruṣottamākhyepaśyedamuṃsuravaraṃpuruṣottamaṃca 55
iti śrīpadmapurāṇekriyāyogasārepuruṣottamamāhatmyaṃnāmaaṣṭādaśo'dhyāyaḥ 18

ekonaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
vyāsauvāca
nārāyaṇaprapannāyenarābhaktisamanvitāḥ
kadācidaśubhaṃteṣāṃvidyatenadvijottama 1
punarevapravakṣyāmimāhātmyaṃkamalāpateḥ
yacchrutvāmānavāḥ sarvelabhaṃteparamaṃpadam 2
vāsudevasyamāhātmyaṃśrutvātṛpyaṃtivaiṣṇavāḥ
pākhaṇḍānahitṛpyaṃtinarakekleśabhāginaḥ 3
pākhaṇḍānāṃsamīpetuviṣṇumāhātmyamuttamam
navaktavyaṃdvijaśreṣṭhavaktavyaṃvaiṣṇavāgrataḥ 4
pūrvaṃ tretāyugevipraurvīśurnāmajaimine
āsītpāparatonityaṃdharmaniṃdāparāyaṇaḥ 5
brahmasvahārīviprendra parastrīgamanodyataḥ
gomāṃsāśīsurāpīcaveśyāvibhramalolupaḥ 6
śaraṇāgatahaṃtācaparaniṃdākaraḥsadā
viśvāsaghātīmitraghnojñātipīḍākarastathā 7
asatyabhāṣīkrūraścapākhaṃḍajanasaṅgabhāk

idānīṃvacmidānāniniśāmayasamāhitaḥ 1
dānaṃtapodvayormadhyedānamekaṃparaṃsmṛtam
tapasāpāpamityuktaṃnapāpodānakarmaṇi 2
tapaḥkṛtayugeśreṣṭhaṃtretāyāṃdhyānamevaca

brāhmaṇāvūcatuḥ

vānaprasthobrahmacārībhikṣuścaiṣāṃprapūjanāt

gṛhiṇāṃparamodharmaḥ proktaścātithipūjanam
āśramācārato'bhraṣṭāstatastegṛhiṇomatāḥ 29
vaṃhatyatithipūjāyāṃdakṣatāṃgṛhiṇoyadi
tadāprayojanaṃteṣāṃkimanyaiḥpuṇyakarmabhiḥ 30
yasyanaśrūyatenāmanacagotraṃnacasthitiḥ
akasmādgṛhamāgacchetso'tithiḥprocyatebudhaiḥ 31
brāhmaṇāḥkṣatriyāvāpivaiśyāvāvṛṣalāstathā

cāṇḍālapramukhāye'nyehīnavarṇasamudbhavāḥ
viṣṇuvatpūjitavyāstepādyārghyairbhūribhojanaiḥ 33
samāgateṣvatithiṣuprayāṇaṃkurutegṛhī
apisatvarayādadyātpādyārghyādīnicadvijaḥ 34
kuryāccakuśalaṃpraśnaṃvacanaiḥ komalākṣaraiḥ
kārayedbhojanaṃvāpidivyaratnairmudāgṛhī 35
sukhademandiretasyaśayanaṃkārayedvudhaḥ
prātarjigamiṣuṃdṛṣṭvātamāyāṃtaṃvivarjayet 36
yadikarmavipākenagṛhībhavativittavān
atithyenātithiḥpūjyastadahaṃvacmisattamau 37
samāgateṣvatithiṣubhaktyādadyāttṛṇādikam
tṛṇābhāvenavaibrūyādbhūbhautiṣṭhetibhaktitaḥ 38
pādaprakṣālanārthaṃtudadyādudakamuttamam
atomadhurayāvācāpṛccheccakuśalādikam 39
phalādikaṃtatodadyādbhaktyābhojanahetave
tadabhāvenamatimānmudāvipraprakāśayet 40
vadeccāhaṃmahāpāpīdaridra pravaro'tithe
karttumicchāmibhaktiṃtedaivataṃtraṃvirodhakam 41
anenavidhinādīnaḥsatyaktvātithipūjanam
svācārapatitonānyoyathoktaṃphalamāpnuyāt 42
anarcito'tithiryasyagacchedvaigṛhiṇogṛhāt
janmakoṭyarjitaṃpuṇyaṃtasyagacchatisaṃkṣayam 43
ekaevātithiryenabhaktibhāvenapūjyate
harettasyahariḥsadyopātakaṃkoṭijanmajam 44
satyaṃvacmihitaṃvacmidṛḍhaṃvacmiprayatnataḥ
vinātithisaparyābhirgṛhiṇonāstivāgatiḥ 45
satyaṃsatyaṃpunaḥsatyamāgaṃtupūjayāvinā
gatirnāstyevanāstyevanāstyevagṛhidharmiṇām 46
jñātidharmaitikhyātovallabhodvāpareyuge
babhūvasarvadharmajñastasya śrīvallabhāhvayā 47
tenasarvāṇikarmāṇikṛtānijñātisevinā
saurāṣṭrevasatiṃcakretayāsahasabhāryayā 48
tatradurgrahasaṃcārāddvādaśābdaṃcavāsavaḥ
navavarṣāṃbutenāsīddurbhikṣaṃsumahaddivajau 49
tasminmahatidurbhikṣelokāstaddeśavāsinaḥ
babhūvurduḥkhitāḥsarvemaryādāmapitatyajuḥ 50
jñānabhadro mahāyogīyugedvāpara saṃjñake
durbhikṣahṛtasaṃpattirbabhūvātyantaduḥkhitaḥ 51
kṣudhākulānsutāndṛṣṭvādārāṃścadvijasaṃmatau
phalamūlādanārthāyajagāmotpatyakāṃgireḥ 52
girerupatyakāprāṃtecirāyuḥsabubhukṣitaḥ
kūṣmāṇḍaphalamekaṃcalebhebrāhmaṇasattamau 53
samādāyaphalaṃtaccaharṣito'saunijālayam
javairjagāmaviprendro jñānabhadro mahāyaśāḥ 54
etasminnantareviprameghairnnīlapadairiva
āvṛtegaganevṛṣṭirmahāsārairbabhūvaha 55
samuniścatayāvṛṣṭyāplāvitākhilavigraha
vanādvanacaraḥ kaścicchītārtaścāyayaugṛham 56
dṛṣṭvātithiṃtuśītārtaṃvavaṃdeśirasātataḥ
dadautṛṇāsanaṃbhaktyātasmaipādyādikaṃtataḥ 57
tatomadhurayāvācātenaivātithināsaha
tasthausvasthenamanasāprajñālāpaṃprakurvatā 58
gṛhiṇyāsahagopenasvāmisevāsudakṣayā
tataḥ kūṣmāṇḍakaṃnavyaṃpakvamatyaṃtayatnataḥ 59
saṃprāpyaharṣitāsādhvīdadaubhāgaṃvidhāyasā
athāsaudurbalogopodinaviṃśamupoṣaṇāt 60
ātitheyomahābhāgaṃdadaucātithayemudā
tatastadgṛhiṇīsādhvīsvāmibhaktiparāyaṇā 61
dadausāpatitaṃbhāgaṃtasmaicātithayemudā
athātithestayostatradaṃpatyoḥsumahātmanoḥ 62
abhūdbhāgadvayaṃbhuktvāsuprītodvijasattama
viṣṇuvatpūjitastābhyāṃso'tithirdṛḍhabhaktitaḥ 63
viśrāmaṃrātrautatraivaprātaḥsnātvāciraṃyayau
gataivamupavāsenadinānāmekaviṃśatiḥ 64
daṃpatītaumahātmānaupaṃcatāṃyayatustataḥ
tenapuṇyaprabhāveṇadaṃpatītaumahāśayau 65
prāpaturviṣṇusāyujyaṃdurllabhaṃyogināmapi
tayoḥpuṇyaprabhāveṇavihitātithipūjayā 66
tasminrājyetudurbhikṣaṃvinaṣṭamabhavattataḥ
atyaṃtasukhinolokāḥ śokavyādhivivarjitāḥ 67
dhanadhānyādisampannābabhūvurdharmatatparāḥ
vinaṣṭādasyavastatranṛpo'bhūllokapālakaḥ 68
nijācāraratālokājaladāḥkāmavarṣiṇaḥ
pūrvajāḥkoṭipuruṣāstathaivāparajāstayoḥ 69
tenaivakarmaṇāmuktiṃjagmuḥpāpavivarjitāḥ
nirdoṣādhanasaṃpannāḥsarvalokaikapūjitāḥ 70
śokavyādhivihīnācavavṛdhesaṃtatistayoḥ

yuvayoḥ prītayorvipraukimanyacchrotumicchatha
vyāsauvāca
itibruvativaitasmim̐ llomaśetapasāṃdhane 72
kālagrastaḥ kṛṣṇaākhustatrottasthaubilānnijāt

pavitrastatracottasthauvadannitimuhurmuhuḥ
ayaṃpāpāśayoduṣṭomūṣakoniśicāśramam 74
khanatetīkṣṇadaṃtaughairgṛhadra vyaṃcakṛṃtati
sarveṣāmevavarṇānāṃkṛpāśreṣṭhāprakīrtitā 75
sācasarveṣukartavyānacaduṣṭeṣujaṃtuṣu
ityuktvāsaumūṣikaṃtaṃdvijaśreṣṭhakṛtainasam 76
nārācenātitīkṣṇenaprāptakālaṃjaghānaha

papātabhūmauviprarṣevyathayāhatacetanaḥ
ākhaunipatitetasmindayālurdvijasattamaḥ 78
hāhākāraṃtataḥkṛtvāsamuttasthaujavenasaḥ
nijakarṇātsamānīyatulasīpatramuttamam 79
tasyākhorvadaneśīrṣekarṇayoścapradattavān
mātastutulasīdevigogindahlādakāriṇi 80

ityuktvāsadvijaśreṣṭhasarvalokopakārakaḥ 81
harenārāyaṇānantaityuccairakaroddhvanim
tulasīpatrasaṃsparśānmūṣakovītakalmaṣaḥ 82

tatodūtāmahāviṣṇoḥ sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutāḥ 83
ājagmuḥsurathaiḥ śīghraṃnetuṃtaṃgatakalmaṣam

idānīṃbrūhikiṃśrotuṃmahābhāgatvamicchasi 87
iti śrīpadmapurāṇekriyāyogasārepaṃcaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ 25

ṣaḍivaṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
jaiminiruvāca
kalauyugemahābhāgasamāyātesudāruṇe
bhaviṣyaṃtijanāḥsarvekīdṛśāstadvadasvame 1
vyāsauvāca
ādyaṃsatyayugaṃprāhustatraviprādayojanāḥ
nārāyaṇārcanaparāḥ śokavyādhivivarjitāḥ 2
satyoktibhāṣiṇaḥ sarvesadayādīrghajīvinaḥ
dhanadhānyādisampannāhiṃsādaṃbhavivarjitāḥ 3
paropakaraṇāścaivasarvaśāstravidastathā
evaṃvidhāḥsatyayugesarvelokā dvijottama 4
rājadharmagrāhiṇaścabhūpālājanapālanāḥ
ahosatyayugasyāstikovyākhyātuṃguṇaṃyaśaḥ 5
adharmoccāraṇaṃyatrajanāḥke'pinakurvate
tretāyugesamāyātedharmaḥ pādonakaṃgataḥ 6
alpaśokānvitālokāḥkecitkecidaghāśrayāḥ
viṣṇudhyānaratālokāyajñadānaparāyaṇāḥ 7
varṇāśramācāraratāḥsukhinaḥ svasthacetasaḥ
kṣetrabhūmikṛtaḥśūdrā saḥrvebrāhmaṇasevinaḥ 8
brāhmaṇāścamahātmānovedavedāṅgapāragāḥ
pratigrahanivṛttāścasatyasandhājitendri yāḥ 9
tapovrataratānityaṃdātāroviṣṇusevinaḥ
tretāyugasyāvasānedvāpareyugaāgate 10
dvipādahīnodharmaḥsyātsukhaduḥkhānvitānarāḥ
kecitkecitpāparatāḥ kecitkeciccadharmiṇaḥ 11
kecitkecidguṇairhīnāḥkecitkecinmahāguṇāḥ
atyantaduḥkhinaḥkecitkeciccasukhinastathā 12
pratigrahebrāhmaṇaścakadācitkurutespṛhām
bhūbhujairdhanalobhenakadācitpīḍyateprajā 13
viṣṇupūjāparāviprāḥ śūdrā ścadvijasevinaḥ
yadāyugeyugedharmaḥpādonatāṃgatodvija 14
tadāvyāsoviṣṇurūpīvedabhāgaṃcakāraha
kalauyugecaviprendra sarvapāpaikarmaṃdire 15
ekapādobhaveddharmaḥ sarvepāparatājanāḥ
brāhmaṇāḥkṣattriyāvaiśyāḥśūdrā pāḥpaparāyaṇāḥ 16
atyaṃtakāminaḥkrūrābhaviṣyaṃtikalauyuge
vedaniṃdākarāścaivadyūtacauryakarāstathā 17
vidhavāsaṃgamagnāścabhaviṣyaṃtikalauyuge
vṛttyarthabrāhmaṇāḥkecinmahākapaṭadharmiṇaḥ 18
sarvestraiṇābhaviṣyaṃtimādakadra vyasevinaḥ
sadā strīyoniniratāḥparadra vyaṃharaṃtica 19
parānnalolupānityaṃtapovrataparāṅmukhāḥ
pākhaṇḍasaṅgabaddhāścabhaviṣyaṃtikalauyuge 20
raktāmbarābhaviṣyaṃtibrāhmaṇāḥ śūdra dharmiṇaḥ
kalauyāsyaṃtinirvṛttāuttamāatinīcatām 21
nīcāścadhanasampannāyāsyaṃtyuccapadaṃprati
pradāsyaṃtyupakāribhyodānānisakalājanāḥ 22
yatnādapicaneṣyaṃtivṛṣalāvipravarttanam
mitrasnehādvadiṣyaṃtikūṭasākṣyaṃkalaujanāḥ 23
adharmabuddhilapanādharmabuddhivilāpinaḥ
parokṣaniṃdakāḥkrūrāḥ saṃmukhepriyavādinaḥ 24
sādhvīvādaṃvadiṣyaṃtibhartāraṃpuṃścalīstriyaḥ
parastrīhiṃsakāścaivagotravikrayiṇodvijāḥ 25
kanyāvikrayiṇaścaivabhaviṣyaṃtikalauyuge
strījitāḥ puruṣāḥsarvestriyo'pyatyaṃtacañcalāḥ 26
kalauyugebhaviṣyaṃtikalaumartyādurāśayāḥ
alpasasyāvasumatīmeghāḥsvalpodakāstathā 27
akālavarṣiṇaścāpibhaviṣyaṃtikalauyuge
kalauviḍbhojinogāvaḥ svalpakṣīrāścajaimine 28
ghṛtahīnaṃcatatkṣīraṃbhaviṣyaṃtinasaṃśayaḥ
ātmastutiparālokāḥ paraniṃdāparāyaṇāḥ 29
bhaviṣyaṃticakharvāṅgābālābahvannabhojanāḥ
pitṛyajñaṃkariṣyaṃtidaṃbhārthaṃbrāhmaṇāḥkalau 30
sarvevacaḥsnehinaḥsyuryāvatkāryaṃnasiddhyti
narāndharmaparāndṛṣṭvāsarvecopahasaṃtivai 31
vardhaṃtyadharmatolokāstasmātpāparatājanāḥ
daśadvādaśavarṣecasamūlo'pyetisaṃkṣayam 32
jalasyevabhaviṣyaṃtiyathāvarṣāsuvṛddhayaḥ
tato lokābhaviṣyaṃtikalaugalitayauvanāḥ 33
paṃcamevāpiṣaṣṭhevāvarṣestrīgarbhadhāriṇī
bahvapatyāścapuruṣābhaviṣyaṃtyatiduḥkhinaḥ 34
netukāmāścasarve'pidātukāmānake'pica
kalaumlecchābhaviṣyaṃtirājānaḥ pāpatatparāḥ 35
ekavarṇābhaviṣyaṃtiviṣayārthaṃkalaujanāḥ
kaleḥprathamasaṃdhyāyāṃhariṃniṃdaṃtimānavāḥ 36
kalermadhyenapaśyaṃtiharernāmānikevale
brāhmaṇāḥkṣattriyāvaiśyāvṛṣalāścakalauyuge 37
ekavarṇābhaviṣyaṃtivarṇāścatvāraevaca
yadāyadādvijaśreṣṭhahāniḥ sukṛtināṃbhavet 38
vṛddhiścapāpināṃnṝṇāṃjñeyāvṛddhistadākalau
yadyapyayaṃkalirghoromayāproktodvijottama 39
tathāpyastimahānasyaguṇoguṇavatāṃvara
satyedvādaśabhirvarṣairbhavetpuṇyasyasādhanam 40
tadardhenacatretāyāṃmāsenadvāparebhavet
ahorātreṇaivaviprabhavettaccakalauyuge 41
tasmātkaliyugenṝṇāṃmartyenaivottamāgatiḥ
dvādaśābdairyuge'nyasminharimabhyarcyayatphalam 42
tatphalaṃlabhatemartyoharimuccāryavaikalau
harernāmaikamapyatrakalauvadatiyonaraḥ 43
kalirnabādhatetaṃcasatyaṃsatyaṃnasaṃśayaḥ
jaiminiruvāca
manaḥśuddhivihīnatvātsamastaṃkarmaniṣphalam 44
itipūrvaṃtvayāproktaṃmanovismayadaṃmama
kalausarvebhaviṣyaṃtimanaḥśuddhivivarjitāḥ 45
teṣāṃyathābhavetkarmasakalaṃbrūhitadguro
vyāsa uvāca
yatkiṃcitkurutekarmamartyodharmaṃkalauyuge 46
tadarpayenmahāviṣṇaubhaktibhāvasamanvitaḥ
viṣṇausamarpitaṃkarmasarvamevākṣayaṃbhavet 47
vyāsauvāca
ititekathitaṃsarvaṃvṛttaṃbrāhmaṇasattama
yacchrutvābhaktibhāvenanaromokṣamavāpnuyāt 48
sūtauvāca
evaṃprabodhitastenajaiminiḥ paramātmanā
kriyāyogaratobhūtvājagāmaparamaṃpadam 49
imaṃkriyāyogasāraṃvyāsenoktaṃmahātmanā
yepaṭhaṃtijanābhaktyāśṛṇvaṃticamumukṣavaḥ 50
tesarvepātakairghorairbahujanmārjitairapi
vimuktāḥparamāṃmuktiṃlabhaṃtenātrasaṃśayaḥ 51
yadyadiṣṭaṃpaṭhaṃtyetacchṛṇvanticamumukṣavaḥ
labhaṃtetattadevāśuprasādātkamalāpateḥ 52
ślokārddhaṃślokamekaṃvāślokapādamathāpivā
naraḥpaṭhitvā śrutvācalabhatevāṃchitaṃphalam 53
likhitvālekhayitvāvāyaḥ śāstramidamarcayet
saviṣṇupūjanasyaivaphalaṃprāpnotimānavaḥ 54
idamatiśayaguhyaṃniḥsṛtaṃvyāsavaktrād (??) ciratarapurāṇaṃprītidaṃvaiṣṇavānām
ciramamaravarādyairvaṃditāṃghrermurāreḥsakalabhuvanabharttuścakriṇaḥ prītaye'stu 55
iti śrīpadmapurāṇekriyāyogasārakhaṇḍevyāsajaiminisaṃvādeyugadharmanirūpaṇa
pūrvakaṃpurāṇamāhātmyavarṇananāmaṣaḍviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ 26
